- **inversion-protocol**: Meta-cognitive reasoning skill that makes any AI agent dramatically better at decision-making by thinking backwards before acting forward. Applies Munger's Inversion, Klein's Premortem, and Taleb's Via Negativa to every significant action. Zero dependencies, zero cost, pure reasoning enhancement. Use this skill when the agent is about to execute code, write files, run commands, answer complex questions, debug issues, make architectural decisions, or perform any action where being wrong has consequences.
